Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the laws framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 187. The resulting operator achieves bound O(53/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the laws decomposition. By minimality, the element a^187 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 53-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
